  PC Agent-E augments 312 human computer-use trajectories with alternative action decisions synthesized by Claude 3.7 Sonnet. It studies training with this combination and introduces WindowsAgentArena-V2 as an updated evaluation benchmark.
- [Scaling Computer-Use Grounding via User Interface Decomposition and Synthesis](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2505-13227/) — 2025-05-19
  Introduces OSWorld-G grounding evaluation and the Jedi synthesis pipeline for examples spanning layout reasoning and fine-grained interaction.
- [The Hidden Dangers of Browsing AI Agents](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2505-13076/) — 2025-05-19
  Outlines an end-to-end threat model and empirical security evaluation across architectural layers of autonomous browser agents.
- [From Assistants to Adversaries: Exploring the Security Risks of Mobile LLM Agents](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2505-12981/) — 2025-05-19
  Analyzes security vulnerabilities across OEM system-level, third-party, and research mobile LLM agents, covering GUI and cross-application attack surfaces.
- [GEM: Gaussian Embedding Modeling for Out-of-Distribution Detection in GUI Agents](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2505-12842/) — 2025-05-19
  GEM detects instructions outside a GUI agent’s capabilities by modeling distances in its input-embedding space with a Gaussian mixture. Experiments across mobile, computer and browser data also examine selective escalation to a cloud model when an input is detected as unfamiliar.
- [Mobile-Bench-v2: A More Realistic and Comprehensive Benchmark for VLM-based Mobile Agents](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2505-11891/) — 2025-05-17
  Mobile-Bench-v2 evaluates mobile GUI agents using multiple acceptable offline action paths, noisy interfaces with ads or pop-ups, and ambiguous instructions with scripted clarification exchanges. These splits separate step-level execution, robustness, and proactive interaction.
- [Characterizing Unintended Consequences in Human-GUI Agent Collaboration for Web Browsing](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2505-09875/) — 2025-05-15
  Studies unintended consequences of collaborative web browsing with GUI agents through social-media analysis and interviews. It organizes observed failures, user impacts, and mitigation practices around planning, interface interaction, feedback, privacy, and trust.
- [Seed1.5-VL Technical Report](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2505-07062/) — 2025-05-11
  Reports Seed1.5-VL, a general vision-language model evaluated on GUI control and gameplay alongside visual understanding and reasoning tasks.
- [AgentVigil: Generic Black-Box Red-teaming for Indirect Prompt Injection against LLM Agents](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2505-05849/) — 2025-05-09
  Introduces AgentVigil, a black-box fuzzing framework for finding indirect prompt injections against LLM agents, including attacks that redirect web navigation.
- [Do MLLMs Capture How Interfaces Guide User Behavior? A Benchmark for Multimodal UI/UX Design Understanding](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2505-05026/) — 2025-05-08
  Introduces WiserUI-Bench, pairing real UI variants from industry A/B tests with observed behavioral winners and expert explanations. It evaluates models’ ability to predict the more effective interface and explain its effect on user behavior.
- [Deep Research Bench: Evaluating AI Web Research Agents](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2506-06287/) — 2025-05-06
  Introduces Deep Research Bench, 89 multi-step web research tasks and a frozen collection of scraped pages for evaluating search agents and their traces over time.
- [WebGen-Bench: Evaluating LLMs on Generating Interactive and Functional Websites from Scratch](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2505-03733/) — 2025-05-06
  Introduces WebGen-Bench for evaluating agents that generate multi-file websites, using a web-navigation agent to check whether the resulting pages pass interaction tests.
- [OSUniverse: Benchmark for Multimodal GUI-navigation AI Agents](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2505-03570/) — 2025-05-06
  Introduces OSUniverse, a desktop GUI benchmark spanning precise clicks through multistep and multiapplication tasks. It provides both manual scoring and an automated validation mechanism.
- [Toward a Human-Centered Evaluation Framework for Trustworthy LLM-Powered GUI Agents](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2504-17934/) — 2025-04-24
  Presents a position paper on privacy and security evaluation for GUI agents, identifying risk categories and challenges in involving human evaluators. It proposes risk-aware assessment and in-context consent as design directions.
- [WASP: Benchmarking Web Agent Security Against Prompt Injection Attacks](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2504-18575/) — 2025-04-22
  Introduces WASP, an end-to-end benchmark for prompt-injection security in web agents. Its evaluation separates partial attack progress from completion of the attacker’s full objective.
- [DoomArena: A framework for Testing AI Agents Against Evolving Security Threats](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2504-14064/) — 2025-04-18
  Presents a modular security testing framework that plugs into BrowserGym and tool-use benchmarks to evaluate agents under configurable threat models.
- [LearnAct: Few-Shot Mobile GUI Agent with a Unified Demonstration Benchmark](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2504-13805/) — 2025-04-18
  LearnAct parses human demonstrations into reusable knowledge, retrieves relevant examples, and uses them during mobile GUI task execution. The accompanying LearnGUI benchmark includes 2,252 offline tasks and 101 online tasks for studying adaptation from demonstrations.
- [WebLists: Extracting Structured Information From Complex Interactive Websites Using Executable LLM Agents](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2504-12682/) — 2025-04-17
  Introduces WebLists, a benchmark for structured extraction from interactive websites, and BardeenAgent, which turns web-agent runs into replayable CSS-selector programs.
- [VLM-Fuzz: Vision Language Model Assisted Recursive Depth-first Search Exploration for Effective UI Testing of Android Apps](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2504-11675/) — 2025-04-16
  VLM-Fuzz combines depth-first UI exploration with Android manifest analysis, runtime widget hierarchies, and on-demand visual reasoning to generate app test inputs. It evaluates coverage on 59 benchmark apps and uses additional Google Play apps to study crash discovery.
- [REAL: Benchmarking Autonomous Agents on Deterministic Simulations of Real Websites](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2504-11543/) — 2025-04-15
  Introduces REAL, a benchmark of deterministic website replicas with 112 tasks covering information retrieval and state-changing browser actions.
- [The Obvious Invisible Threat: LLM-Powered GUI Agents' Vulnerability to Fine-Print Injections](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2504-11281/) — 2025-04-15
  Characterizes six forms of GUI-content injection and studies their effects on GUI agents and human users using adversarial webpages. The analysis examines privacy failures and the limits of human oversight.
- [UI-E2I-Synth: Advancing GUI Grounding with Large-Scale Instruction Synthesis](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2504-11257/) — 2025-04-15
  UI-E2I-Synth uses GPT-4o to synthesize GUI grounding instructions with varied complexity, addressing small elements, imbalanced element types, and implicit requests. The accompanying UI-I2E-Bench evaluates instruction-to-element localization across these annotation challenges.
- [RealWebAssist: A Benchmark for Long-Horizon Web Assistance with Real-World Users](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2504-10445/) — 2025-04-14
  RealWebAssist evaluates web assistance through sequences of instructions collected from real users across multiple websites. It tests intent interpretation, user-specific routines, long-term context, and visual grounding when instructions are ambiguous or evolve during interaction.
- [Breaking the Data Barrier -- Building GUI Agents Through Task Generalization](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2504-10127/) — 2025-04-14
  Studies whether mid-training on GUI perception, multimodal reasoning, and text reasoning transfers to GUI-agent planning. Experiments compare 11 task sources and curated mixtures using WebArena and AndroidWorld to investigate training-data choices when GUI trajectories are scarce.
- [AgentA/B: Automated and Scalable Web A/BTesting with Interactive LLM Agents](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2504-09723/) — 2025-04-13
  Introduces AgentA/B, which uses persona-based LLM agents to navigate real webpages and perform shopping interactions for simulated web A/B tests.
- [AgentRewardBench: Evaluating Automatic Evaluations of Web Agent Trajectories](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2504-08942/) — 2025-04-11
  AgentRewardBench evaluates automated web-agent judges against expert reviews of 1,302 trajectories from five benchmarks. The annotations cover completion, side effects, and repetition, enabling comparison of LLM judges and rule-based task evaluators.
- [Kimi-VL Technical Report](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2504-07491/) — 2025-04-10
  Presents Kimi-VL, a general vision-language model evaluated on multi-turn agent tasks including OSWorld and on ScreenSpot-Pro alongside other vision-language tasks.
- [On the Robustness of GUI Grounding Models Against Image Attacks](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2504-04716/) — 2025-04-07
  Evaluates GUI grounding models under natural image noise, untargeted adversarial perturbations, and targeted image attacks. Experiments cover mobile, desktop, and web interfaces and analyze sensitivity to image resolution and adversarial changes.
- [ScreenSpot-Pro: GUI Grounding for Professional High-Resolution Computer Use](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2504-07981/) — 2025-04-04
  Evaluates GUI target localization in high-resolution professional software and studies cascaded visual search to narrow the target region.
- [ReuseDroid: A VLM-empowered Android UI Test Migrator Boosted by Active Feedback](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2504-02357/) — 2025-04-03
  ReuseDroid migrates Android GUI tests between functionally similar apps using VLM agents that separate shared test intent from app-specific interaction flows. It introduces LinPro, a dataset of 578 migration tasks across 39 apps, to evaluate test transfer.
- [Are Autonomous Web Agents Good Testers?](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2504-01495/) — 2025-04-02
  Adapts web agents to execute natural-language test steps, verify assertions, and issue test verdicts. The study introduces 113 passing and failing test cases across three offline web applications and compares two agent implementations with qualitative failure analysis.
- [An Illusion of Progress? Assessing the Current State of Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2504-01382/) — 2025-04-02
  Introduces Online-Mind2Web, with 300 tasks across 136 live websites, and examines how benchmark design affects assessments of web agents. It also develops an LLM judge for task completion and compares its judgments with human evaluation.
- [Navi-plus: Managing Ambiguous GUI Navigation Tasks with Follow-up Questions](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2503-24180/) — 2025-03-31
  Navi-plus evaluates GUI agents that ask follow-up questions to complete ambiguous user instructions. It provides GUI clarification question-answer pairs and Dual-Stream Trajectory Evaluation for this interactive setting.
- [sudo rm -rf agentic_security](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2503-20279/) — 2025-03-26
  Evaluates security failures of computer-use agents under adversarial requests and screen-context manipulation. The study examines refusal behavior while agents perform tasks in desktop and web environments.
- [Agents in the Sandbox: End-to-End Crash Bug Reproduction for Minecraft](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2503-20036/) — 2025-03-25
  Introduces BugCraft, which turns Minecraft crash reports into steps and has a vision-based agent execute them through a macro API; BugCraft-Bench supports evaluation.
- [Agent-Initiated Interaction in Phone UI Automation](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2503-19537/) — 2025-03-25
  Defines when phone agents should request user interaction and introduces AndroidInteraction annotations for deciding whether and how to engage the user.
- [Does Chain-of-Thought Reasoning Help Mobile GUI Agent? An Empirical Study](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2503-16788/) — 2025-03-21
  This study compares matched reasoning and non-reasoning vision-language models on static mobile GUI tasks and interactive AndroidWorld navigation. It finds that chain-of-thought effects vary across tasks and model families.
- [UI-Vision: A Desktop-centric GUI Benchmark for Visual Perception and Interaction](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2503-15661/) — 2025-03-19
  Provides annotated desktop demonstrations for evaluating element grounding, layout grounding, and action prediction.
- [STEVE: A Step Verification Pipeline for Computer-use Agent Training](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2503-12532/) — 2025-03-16
  Labels individual computer-use actions by comparing surrounding screenshots, then trains agents from both successful and unsuccessful steps.
- [AgentDAM: Privacy Leakage Evaluation for Autonomous Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2503-09780/) — 2025-03-12
  Evaluates whether autonomous web agents minimize use of sensitive personal information when completing tasks that expose unnecessary private data.
- [In-Context Defense in Computer Agents: An Empirical Study](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2503-09241/) — 2025-03-12
  Studies in-context defenses for computer agents exposed to misleading pop-ups and other environmental instructions. It examines defensive reasoning before action planning and how a small set of examples changes resistance to these attacks.
- [BEARCUBS: A benchmark for computer-using web agents](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2503-07919/) — 2025-03-10
  BEARCUBS provides 111 live-web information-seeking questions with short answers and human-validated browsing trajectories. Its tasks require multimodal computer interaction, including video understanding and navigation of interactive 3D content.
- [FedMABench: Benchmarking Mobile Agents on Decentralized Heterogeneous User Data](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2503-05143/) — 2025-03-07
  Introduces a benchmark for federated mobile-agent training under heterogeneous user and application distributions, combining datasets, algorithms, and base models.
- [SafeArena: Evaluating the Safety of Autonomous Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2503-04957/) — 2025-03-06
  Pairs benign and harmful web tasks to assess deliberate misuse risks and categorize agent behavior by risk level.
- [AutoEval: A Practical Framework for Autonomous Evaluation of Mobile Agents](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2503-02403/) — 2025-03-04
  Generates reward signals from UI-state changes and uses a judging system to evaluate mobile agents with reduced manual evaluator construction.
- [Why Are Web AI Agents More Vulnerable Than Standalone LLMs? A Security Analysis](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2502-20383/) — 2025-02-27
  Analyzes web-agent vulnerabilities at the component level, examining goal placement in prompts, multistep action generation, and observations as sources of increased susceptibility.
- [WebGames: Challenging General-Purpose Web-Browsing AI Agents](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2502-18356/) — 2025-02-25
  Introduces WebGames, a browser-agent benchmark with 50+ interactive challenges in a self-contained test environment and verifiable solutions.
- [Towards Sustainable Web Agents: A Plea for Transparency and Dedicated Metrics for Energy Consumption](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2502-17903/) — 2025-02-25
  Examines energy and carbon-accounting limitations in web-agent evaluation and argues for transparent, dedicated sustainability measurements.
- [Towards Enterprise-Ready Computer Using Generalist Agent](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2503-01861/) — 2025-02-24
  Describes the CUGA enterprise-agent development process, emphasizing iterative evaluation, failure analysis, and refinement on software-task benchmarks.
- [Programming with Pixels: Can Computer-Use Agents do Software Engineering?](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2502-18525/) — 2025-02-24
  Introduces a visual IDE environment and benchmark for software-engineering tasks, comparing screenshot-based control with access to editing and shell tools.
- [Evaluating the Robustness of Multimodal Agents Against Active Environmental Injection Attacks](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2502-13053/) — 2025-02-18
  Examines active environmental injection against mobile agents, testing whether adversarial interface content redirects decisions during task execution.
- [UXAgent: An LLM Agent-Based Usability Testing Framework for Web Design](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2502-12561/) — 2025-02-18
  Introduces UXAgent, which uses simulated users and a browser connector to test website usability and present their actions, responses, and recordings to UX researchers.
- [WorldGUI: An Interactive Benchmark for Desktop GUI Automation from Any Starting Point](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2502-08047/) — 2025-02-12
  Introduces desktop and web tasks with varied initial states to test planning recovery and adaptability beyond canonical starting configurations.
- [InSTA: Towards Internet-Scale Training For Agents](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2502-06776/) — 2025-02-10
  InSTA builds a pipeline that creates website tasks, collects web-agent trajectories and filters them by judged success to train smaller web navigation agents.
- [CowPilot: A Framework for Autonomous and Human-Agent Collaborative Web Navigation](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2501-16609/) — 2025-01-28
  Introduces CowPilot, a web-navigation framework where agents suggest actions and users can pause, reject, override, or resume them. It evaluates autonomous and collaborative use through task success and human effort.
- [WinClick: GUI Grounding with Multimodal Large Language Models](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2503-04730/) — 2025-01-27
  Introduces a screenshot-based Windows GUI agent and the WinSpot benchmark for evaluating localization of actionable interface regions.
- [WebWalker: Benchmarking LLMs in Web Traversal](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2501-07572/) — 2025-01-13
  Introduces WebWalkerQA and an exploration-and-critique framework for gathering information by traversing multiple levels of a website.
- [Beyond Pass or Fail: Multi-Dimensional Benchmarking of Foundation Models for Goal-based Mobile UI Navigation](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2501-02863/) — 2025-01-06
  Sphinx benchmarks foundation models on goal-based mobile UI navigation, using app test cases and a toolkit that identifies different failure modes.
- [A3: Android Agent Arena for Mobile GUI Agents with Essential-State Procedural Evaluation](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2501-01149/) — 2025-01-02
  Introduces Android Agent Arena with online application tasks and procedural evaluation based on reaching essential intermediate states.
- [Are CAPTCHAs Still Bot-hard? Generalized Visual CAPTCHA Solving with Agentic Vision Language Model](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-6942e8211a0f59eb7878/) — 2025
  Presents Halligan, a VLM agent that abstracts visual CAPTCHAs into frames, elements and keypoints and solves each as a search problem through generated interaction code, tested on 2,600 challenges of 26 types.
- [GUI Testing Arena: A Unified Benchmark for Advancing Autonomous GUI Testing Agent](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2412-18426/) — 2024-12-24
  Evaluates GUI testing through test-intent generation, execution, and defect detection using real, modified, and synthetic mobile application data.
- [TheAgentCompany: Benchmarking LLM Agents on Consequential Real World Tasks](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2412-14161/) — 2024-12-18
  Simulates a software-company workplace to evaluate agents on professional tasks involving websites, programs, files, and coworker communication.
- [Large Action Models: From Inception to Implementation](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2412-10047/) — 2024-12-13
  Uses a Windows-agent case study to describe a development workflow for large action models, covering data collection, training, grounding, environment integration, and evaluation.
- [The BrowserGym Ecosystem for Web Agent Research](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2412-05467/) — 2024-12-06
  Describes BrowserGym's shared web interaction environment and AgentLab tooling for consistent experiments across multiple agent benchmarks.
- [BigDocs: An Open Dataset for Training Multimodal Models on Document and Code Tasks](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2412-04626/) — 2024-12-05
  BigDocs-7.5M provides multimodal document data, while BigDocs-Bench includes GUI reasoning and image-to-code tasks relevant to screen understanding.
- [Generalist Virtual Agents: A Survey on Autonomous Agents Across Digital Platforms](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2411-10943/) — 2024-11-17
  Surveys virtual agents for browsers, applications and operating systems, organizing prior work by task type, observation and action spaces, model strategies and evaluation methods. It examines screen-based interaction and keyboard, mouse and touch control, alongside transferability and long-sequence decision-making challenges.
- [The Dawn of GUI Agent: A Preliminary Case Study with Claude 3.5 Computer Use](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2411-10323/) — 2024-11-15
  Presents a preliminary study of Claude 3.5 Computer Use across desktop tasks and software, analyzing planning, action, and critique behavior and providing a framework for deploying GUI-automation models.
- [Attacking Vision-Language Computer Agents via Pop-ups](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2411-02391/) — 2024-11-04
  Tests whether adversarial visual pop-ups divert computer agents from intended tasks in desktop and web benchmark environments.
- [Foundations and Recent Trends in Multimodal Mobile Agents: A Survey](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2411-02006/) — 2024-11-04
  Surveys multimodal mobile agents, organizing prompt-based and training-based approaches alongside evaluation benchmarks, supporting technologies, and open challenges in mobile task execution.
- [AndroidLab: Training and Systematic Benchmarking of Android Autonomous Agents](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2410-24024/) — 2024-10-31
  Provides a shared Android action environment, reproducible tasks, and instruction data for training and comparing language and multimodal agents.
- [Evaluating Cultural and Social Awareness of LLM Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2410-23252/) — 2024-10-30
  Introduces CASA to assess web agents' responses to cultural and social norms in shopping and forum tasks, including misleading observations.
- [VideoWebArena: Evaluating Long Context Multimodal Agents with Video Understanding Web Tasks](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2410-19100/) — 2024-10-24
  Evaluates whether web agents can retain procedural skills and factual information from long video tutorials and use them during tasks.
- [MobileSafetyBench: Evaluating Safety of Autonomous Agents in Mobile Device Control](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2410-17520/) — 2024-10-23
  Evaluates mobile agents on misuse, side effects, and indirect prompt-injection risks in an Android emulator environment.
- [AdvAgent: Controllable Blackbox Red-teaming on Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2410-17401/) — 2024-10-22
  Introduces a black-box web-agent red-teaming framework that learns adversarial prompts from agent feedback to study security weaknesses.
- [Large Language Models Empowered Personalized Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2410-17236/) — 2024-10-22
  PersonalWAB benchmarks personalized web tasks, while PUMA retrieves relevant past web behavior to adapt agents' instruction handling and actions.
- [Beyond Browsing: API-Based Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2410-16464/) — 2024-10-21
  Compares browsing-only, API-only, and hybrid web agents on WebArena, examining how access to application APIs changes task execution alongside browser interaction.
- [SPA-Bench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for SmartPhone Agent Evaluation](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2410-15164/) — 2024-10-19
  Introduces a smartphone-agent benchmark with English and Chinese tasks, real-time Android interaction, and completion and resource-use metrics.
- [Refusal-Trained LLMs Are Easily Jailbroken As Browser Agents](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2410-13886/) — 2024-10-11
  Introduces BrowserART and studies whether chat-model refusal behavior transfers to browser agents carrying out potentially harmful instructions.
- [From Interaction to Impact: Towards Safer AI Agents Through Understanding and Evaluating Mobile UI Operation Impacts](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2410-09006/) — 2024-10-11
  Develops a taxonomy of mobile-action consequences and annotated interaction data to evaluate whether models recognize impactful or risky UI operations.
- [ST-WebAgentBench: A Benchmark for Evaluating Safety and Trustworthiness in Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2410-06703/) — 2024-10-09
  Evaluates web-task completion under explicit enterprise policies, measuring policy-compliant completion and safety violations separately.
- [Multimodal Auto Validation For Self-Refinement in Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2410-00689/) — 2024-10-01
  Studies text and visual validation for web agents and uses the resulting validator to detect failures and guide workflow self-refinement.
- [Inferring Alt-text For UI Icons With Large Language Models During App Development](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2409-18060/) — 2024-09-26
  Introduces IconDesc, which generates mobile icon alt-text from partial UI context such as element identifiers, bounds, nearby text and hierarchy. Model evaluation and a user study examine the resulting accessibility descriptions.
- [The Impact of Element Ordering on LM Agent Performance](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2409-12089/) — 2024-09-18
  Studies how the ordering of UI elements in an agent observation affects web and desktop performance. A pixel-based element detector and dimensionality-reduction ordering are evaluated on OmniACT when document hierarchy is unavailable.
- [EIA: Environmental Injection Attack on Generalist Web Agents for Privacy Leakage](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2409-11295/) — 2024-09-17
  Evaluates privacy-directed webpage instruction injection against generalist web agents using PII-related interaction steps from Mind2Web, including attempts to expose specific user information or entire requests.
- [Windows Agent Arena: Evaluating Multi-Modal OS Agents at Scale](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2409-08264/) — 2024-09-12
  Adapts real-desktop evaluation to Windows applications and supports parallel execution of benchmark tasks.
- [Guardian: A Runtime Framework for LLM-Based UI Exploration](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-7424d66754ec26d68d83/) — 2024-09-11
  Guardian guides LLM-driven mobile app testing by narrowing available UI actions and replanning after invalidated actions; it is evaluated on 58 tasks from 23 apps.
- [WebQuest: A Benchmark for Multimodal QA on Web Page Sequences](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2409-13711/) — 2024-09-06
  Introduces question answering over individual screens, multiple web pages, and navigation traces to test information synthesis across views.
- [From Grounding to Planning: Benchmarking Bottlenecks in Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2409-01927/) — 2024-09-03
  Separates web-agent planning and grounding evaluations on Mind2Web to analyze which components limit task performance.
- [VisualAgentBench: Towards Large Multimodal Models as Visual Foundation Agents](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2408-06327/) — 2024-08-12
  Benchmarks visual agents across GUI, embodied, and design tasks, with training trajectories collected through solvers, agents, and human demonstrations.
- [Caution for the Environment: Multimodal LLM Agents are Susceptible to Environmental Distractions](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2408-02544/) — 2024-08-05
  Measures how irrelevant GUI content affects multimodal agents using a simulated dataset and three perception patterns. The study also examines adversarial environment injection and possible ways to improve instruction faithfulness.
- [OfficeBench: Benchmarking Language Agents across Multiple Applications for Office Automation](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2407-19056/) — 2024-07-26
  Evaluates language agents on office workflows requiring information integration, long-term planning, and switching between applications.
- [AppWorld: A Controllable World of Apps and People for Benchmarking Interactive Coding Agents](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2407-18901/) — 2024-07-26
  Introduces simulated application APIs and state-based task evaluation for interactive coding agents performing everyday digital workflows.
- [OpenHands: An Open Platform for AI Software Developers as Generalist Agents](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2407-16741/) — 2024-07-23
  OpenHands is a platform for agents that write code, use a command line and browse the web, with sandboxed execution and support for agent evaluation.
- [AssistantBench: Can Web Agents Solve Realistic and Time-Consuming Tasks?](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2407-15711/) — 2024-07-22
  Introduces realistic open-web tasks and the SeePlanAct agent, analyzing the limitations of browsing and retrieval in time-consuming assistant work.
- [Spider2-V: How Far Are Multimodal Agents From Automating Data Science and Engineering Workflows?](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2407-10956/) — 2024-07-15
  Benchmarks multimodal agents on data-science and engineering workflows involving enterprise applications, code, and GUI operations in real computer environments.
- [Systematic Categorization, Construction and Evaluation of New Attacks against Multi-modal Mobile GUI Agents](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2407-09295/) — 2024-07-12
  Develops a threat taxonomy and evaluation framework for attacks against multimodal mobile GUI agents, with case studies and dataset experiments.
- [AUITestAgent: Automatic Requirements Oriented GUI Function Testing](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2407-09018/) — 2024-07-12
  Automates mobile GUI tests from natural-language requirements, combining action generation with verification based on interaction traces.
- [UICrit: Enhancing Automated Design Evaluation with a UICritique Dataset](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2407-08850/) — 2024-07-11
  Introduces designer-authored mobile UI critiques and quality ratings, studying how targeted examples improve automated design feedback.
- [WorkArena++: Towards Compositional Planning and Reasoning-based Common Knowledge Work Tasks](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2407-05291/) — 2024-07-07
  Defines compositional enterprise workflows to evaluate planning and reasoning, with generation of ground-truth interaction traces.
- [Seeing is Believing: Vision-driven Non-crash Functional Bug Detection for Mobile Apps](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2407-03037/) — 2024-07-03
  Trident combines exploration, monitoring, and detection agents to identify functional bugs in a mobile app. It reasons over screenshot sequences and interface transitions to construct test oracles, evaluated on 590 non-crash bugs and additional live apps.
- [CRAB: Cross-environment Agent Benchmark for Multimodal Language Model Agents](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2407-01511/) — 2024-07-01
  Introduces CRAB, a framework for cross-environment agent tasks with graph-based evaluation and a benchmark spanning desktop and phone interactions.
- [Tree Search for Language Model Agents](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2407-01476/) — 2024-07-01
  Applies best-first search to agent actions within interactive web environments, allowing exploration and multistep planning at inference time. WebArena and VisualWebArena experiments examine search behavior and test-time computation.
- [Mobile-Bench: An Evaluation Benchmark for LLM-based Mobile Agents](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2407-00993/) — 2024-07-01
  Evaluates mobile agents using combined UI and API actions, tasks with varying app scope, and checkpoints for intermediate progress.
- [RealWeb: A Benchmark for Universal Instruction Following in Realistic Web Services Navigation](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-a998ab1efc741ba69d82/) — 2024-07
  Introduces RealWeb, a Chinese benchmark of 11,739 instructions on 40 real websites with annotated screenshots and slot trees, plus a baseline that detects page regions, parses instructions and acts on the pages.
- [SpreadsheetBench: Towards Challenging Real World Spreadsheet Manipulation](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2406-14991/) — 2024-06-21
  SpreadsheetBench evaluates LLM spreadsheet manipulation with 912 questions from Excel forums and multiple spreadsheet files as test cases for each instruction.
- [Identifying User Goals from UI Trajectories](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2406-14314/) — 2024-06-20
  Defines goal identification from observed UI trajectories and an evaluation method for comparing intent descriptions within the same interface context. Experiments reuse Android and web automation data to compare human and model inference of users’ detailed goals.
- [E-ANT: A Large-Scale Dataset for Efficient Automatic GUI NavigaTion](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2406-14250/) — 2024-06-20
  Introduces Chinese-language GUI navigation traces and annotated screenshots collected from human interactions with mini-applications.
- [AgentDojo: A Dynamic Environment to Evaluate Prompt Injection Attacks and Defenses for LLM Agents](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2406-13352/) — 2024-06-19
  Introduces AgentDojo, an extensible environment of tool-calling tasks over untrusted data that measures prompt-injection attacks, defenses and utility, with 97 tasks and 629 security test cases.
- [WONDERBREAD: A Benchmark for Evaluating Multimodal Foundation Models on Business Process Management Tasks](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2406-13264/) — 2024-06-19
  Introduces WONDERBREAD, a benchmark pairing human WebArena workflow demonstrations with screen recordings, action logs, and written procedures. Six evaluation tasks cover documentation, knowledge transfer, and process improvement beyond end-to-end execution.
- [Dissecting Adversarial Robustness of Multimodal LM Agents](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2406-12814/) — 2024-06-18
  Introduces adversarial tasks on VisualWebArena and an evaluation framework that tracks how manipulated information passes between agent components. The analysis examines vulnerabilities introduced by reflection and tree-search evaluators as well as direct action generation.
- [WebCanvas: Benchmarking Web Agents in Online Environments](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2406-12373/) — 2024-06-18
  Introduces WebCanvas and Mind2Web-Live, evaluating online task progress through meaningful intermediate states on changing websites.
- [GUI-World: A Video Benchmark and Dataset for Multimodal GUI-oriented Understanding](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2406-10819/) — 2024-06-16
  Introduces video-based GUI questions and annotations for studying temporal interface understanding across varied interaction settings.
- [VideoGUI: A Benchmark for GUI Automation from Instructional Videos](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2406-10227/) — 2024-06-14
  Evaluates GUI automation from instructional videos at separate levels of high-level planning, action narration, and primitive execution.
- [GuardAgent: Safeguard LLM Agents by a Guard Agent via Knowledge-Enabled Reasoning](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2406-09187/) — 2024-06-13
  Introduces GuardAgent, which translates safety requests into executable guardrail code using plans and retrieved demonstrations. Mind2Web-SC supplies a web-agent safety-policy evaluation alongside a separate healthcare access-control benchmark.
- [Practical, Automated Scenario-based Mobile App Testing](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2406-08340/) — 2024-06-12
  ScenTest builds scenario-specific event knowledge graphs from crowdsourced test reports containing GUI and action information. It uses the current app state and testing context to generate scenario tests across applications.
- [MobileAgentBench: An Efficient and User-Friendly Benchmark for Mobile LLM Agents](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2406-08184/) — 2024-06-12
  Introduces a mobile-agent benchmark with tasks across open-source applications, organized by difficulty to reduce manual evaluation effort.
- [On the Effects of Data Scale on UI Control Agents](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2406-03679/) — 2024-06-06
  Studies how Android task performance changes with demonstration scale and releases AndroidControl with high- and low-level instructions.
- [WebSuite: Systematically Evaluating Why Web Agents Fail](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2406-01623/) — 2024-06-01
  WebSuite organizes browser operations into an action taxonomy and tests both isolated controls and complete workflows. Its diagnostic tasks attribute failures to specific actions, enabling comparison of text-based and multimodal web agents.
- [Large Language Models Can Self-Improve At Web Agent Tasks](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2405-20309/) — 2024-05-30
  Examines self-improvement by fine-tuning web agents on three mixtures of model-generated training data. WebArena experiments and trajectory-level evaluation measures study task performance, robustness, and behavior beyond aggregate success rates.
- [AndroidWorld: A Dynamic Benchmarking Environment for Autonomous Agents](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2405-14573/) — 2024-05-23
  Introduces parameterized Android tasks with initialization, success checks, and cleanup for reproducible interactive evaluation.
- [Latent State Estimation Helps UI Agents to Reason](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2405-11120/) — 2024-05-17
  Studies explicit textual estimates of latent UI state, including whether commanded actions occurred and how far a task has progressed. The experiments compare agents that reason with these estimates against agents that omit them across public and internal task benchmarks.
- [Unveiling Disparities in Web Task Handling Between Human and Web Agent](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2405-04497/) — 2024-05-07
  Compares human think-aloud web-task behavior with web-agent planning, action, and reflection. The analysis highlights differences in updating plans, handling ambiguity, and investigating failures.
- [Visual Grounding Methods for Efficient Interaction with Desktop Graphical User Interfaces](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2407-01558/) — 2024-05-05
  Compares modular OCR-based and end-to-end methods for locating desktop GUI targets from natural-language instructions.
- [Navigating WebAI: Training Agents to Complete Web Tasks with Large Language Models and Reinforcement Learning](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2405-00516/) — 2024-05-01
  Combines supervised and reinforcement learning for MiniWoB web tasks and investigates whether agents understand HTML structure or memorize target elements.
- [MMAC-Copilot: Multi-modal Agent Collaboration Operating Copilot](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2404-18074/) — 2024-04-28
  Introduces MMAC-Copilot, which coordinates agents with different expertise to operate PC applications. VIBench evaluates visual interaction with applications that lack usable APIs, spanning office, recreation and game tasks.
- [Benchmarking Mobile Device Control Agents across Diverse Configurations](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2404-16660/) — 2024-04-25
  Introduces B-MoCA, an interactive mobile-control benchmark that varies device settings, layouts, and languages to test generalization.
- [AutoScraper: A Progressive Understanding Web Agent for Web Scraper Generation](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2404-12753/) — 2024-04-19
  Introduces AutoScraper, a two-stage framework that uses HTML structure and similarities across pages to generate scrapers for data collection from websites.
- [UIClip: A Data-driven Model for Assessing User Interface Design](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2404-12500/) — 2024-04-18
  UIClip assesses UI design quality and visual relevance from screenshots and descriptions, using crawled, augmented and human-rated interfaces for training.
- [MMInA: Benchmarking Multihop Multimodal Internet Agents](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2404-09992/) — 2024-04-15
  Evaluates multimodal agents on compositional tasks across changing websites and studies replaying prior trajectories as memory support.
- [Large Language Models for Mobile GUI Text Input Generation: An Empirical Study](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2404-08948/) — 2024-04-13
  Studies language-model generation of mobile text inputs, comparing UI representations, execution feedback, and human assistance for app testing.
- [Enhancing Mobile "How-to" Queries with Automated Search Results Verification and Reranking](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2404-08860/) — 2024-04-13
  Executes instructions from mobile how-to search results inside a controlled Android environment, then uses the observed success of those instructions to verify and rerank technical-support results.
- [LlamaTouch: A Faithful and Scalable Testbed for Mobile UI Task Automation](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2404-16054/) — 2024-04-12
  LlamaTouch executes mobile tasks on devices and evaluates whether an agent visits manually annotated essential application or system states. Screenshot and hierarchy annotations, combined with exact and fuzzy state matching, support evaluation of 496 tasks across multiple agents.
- [OSWorld: Benchmarking Multimodal Agents for Open-Ended Tasks in Real Computer Environments](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2404-07972/) — 2024-04-11
  Introduces a real-computer evaluation environment with execution-based checks for tasks spanning desktop applications and operating-system state.
- [Autonomous Evaluation and Refinement of Digital Agents](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2404-06474/) — 2024-04-09
  Compares automatic trajectory evaluators for web navigation and device control against oracle judgments. The resulting feedback is used for agent fine-tuning and inference-time guidance, including experiments on WebArena.
- [VisualWebBench: How Far Have Multimodal LLMs Evolved in Web Page Understanding and Grounding?](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2404-05955/) — 2024-04-09
  Separately evaluates web-page reading, understanding, and element grounding across tasks drawn from real websites.
- [Draw-and-Understand: Leveraging Visual Prompts to Enable MLLMs to Comprehend What You Want](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2403-20271/) — 2024-03-29
  Introduces Draw-and-Understand and MDVP-Bench for understanding visual prompts such as points and boxes; its multi-domain data includes mobile and web screenshots.
- [AgentStudio: A Toolkit for Building General Virtual Agents](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2403-17918/) — 2024-03-26
  AgentStudio combines an interactive GUI/API environment with tools for creating tasks, annotating interface elements, and labeling actions in videos. Its online suite and GroundUI, IDMBench, and CriticBench datasets examine interaction, grounding, learning from video, and success detection.
- [Tur\[k\]ingBench: A Challenge Benchmark for Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2403-11905/) — 2024-03-18
  Evaluates web agents on multimodal annotation tasks adapted from HTML interfaces originally built for crowdsourcing workers.
- [WorkArena: How Capable Are Web Agents at Solving Common Knowledge Work Tasks?](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2403-07718/) — 2024-03-12
  Introduces enterprise web tasks on ServiceNow and BrowserGym interfaces for evaluating browser-based agents.
- [PPTC-R benchmark: Towards Evaluating the Robustness of Large Language Models for PowerPoint Task Completion](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2403-03788/) — 2024-03-06
  Benchmarks robustness of API-based PowerPoint completion under sentence-level, semantic, and multilingual instruction perturbations and changes to the available API set.
- [SheetAgent: Towards A Generalist Agent for Spreadsheet Reasoning and Manipulation via Large Language Models](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2403-03636/) — 2024-03-06
  Introduces SheetRM, a benchmark of long-horizon spreadsheet tasks, and SheetAgent, an LLM agent that plans, retrieves information and reflects while manipulating spreadsheets.
- [OmniACT: A Dataset and Benchmark for Enabling Multimodal Generalist Autonomous Agents for Desktop and Web](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2402-17553/) — 2024-02-27
  Introduces a dataset and benchmark for generating executable task scripts from screenshots and visually grounded desktop or web instructions.
- [WIPI: A New Web Threat for LLM-Driven Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2402-16965/) — 2024-02-26
  Studies WIPI, a black-box indirect-instruction threat in which webpage content redirects LLM-driven web agents. Evaluation spans plugin-based agents, Web GPTs, and open-source web-agent implementations.
- [On the Multi-turn Instruction Following for Conversational Web Agents](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2402-15057/) — 2024-02-23
  MT-Mind2Web evaluates web navigation through sequences of conversational user instructions. Self-MAP combines memory-augmented planning with reflection to preserve context and dependencies across those interaction turns.
- [Watch Out for Your Agents! Investigating Backdoor Threats to LLM-Based Agents](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2402-11208/) — 2024-02-17
  Studies backdoor attacks on LLM agents, including triggers in user queries or intermediate observations, with web shopping among the evaluated agent tasks.
- [Understanding the Weakness of Large Language Model Agents within a Complex Android Environment](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2402-06596/) — 2024-02-09
  Introduces AndroidArena and analyzes agent limitations in cross-application tasks, user constraints, exploration, and reflection.
- [WebLINX: Real-World Website Navigation with Multi-Turn Dialogue](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2402-05930/) — 2024-02-08
  Collects expert demonstrations of conversational web navigation and studies retrieval-based element selection for processing large pages.
- [WebVoyager: Building an End-to-End Web Agent with Large Multimodal Models](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2401-13919/) — 2024-01-25
  Combines multimodal web interaction with a real-website task collection and an automated evaluation protocol.
- [VisualWebArena: Evaluating Multimodal Agents on Realistic Visual Web Tasks](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2401-13649/) — 2024-01-24
  Introduces visually grounded website tasks for evaluating multimodal agents that must interpret both images and text.
- [AgentBoard: An Analytical Evaluation Board of Multi-turn LLM Agents](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2401-13178/) — 2024-01-24
  Introduces AgentBoard, a multiturn agent evaluation framework with intermediate progress metrics and analysis tools. Its broader task suite includes WebShop and WebArena interactions with stepwise progress measurements.
- [WebVLN: Vision-and-Language Navigation on Websites](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2312-15820/) — 2023-12-25
  WebVLN trains website navigation from question-based instructions using both rendered visual information and underlying HTML. It introduces WebVLN-v1 and a website-aware navigation network to study this digital variant of vision-and-language navigation.
- [ASSISTGUI: Task-Oriented Desktop Graphical User Interface Automation](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2312-13108/) — 2023-12-20
  Introduces Windows application tasks and an actor-critic agent framework for mouse-and-keyboard automation of desktop workflows.
- [Exposing Limitations of Language Model Agents in Sequential-Task Compositions on the Web](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2311-18751/) — 2023-11-30
  CompWoB adds 50 composed web-automation tasks to study generalization from simpler interactions. The paper compares prompted and fine-tuned agents, balances task training data, and probes sensitivity to changes in instruction order.
- [GAIA: a benchmark for General AI Assistants](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2311-12983/) — 2023-11-21
  Introduces questions that test assistant reasoning, multimodal understanding, browsing, and tool use beyond screen-action execution alone.
- [PPTC Benchmark: Evaluating Large Language Models for PowerPoint Task Completion](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2311-01767/) — 2023-11-03
  Evaluates multi-turn PowerPoint creation and editing by scoring the resulting presentation files.
- [InstructExcel: A Benchmark for Natural Language Instruction in Excel](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2310-14495/) — 2023-10-23
  Introduces InstructExcel, a benchmark for generating Excel OfficeScripts from natural-language requests, built using scripts recorded from user actions and a collection of existing spreadsheets.
- [AllTogether: Investigating the Efficacy of Spliced Prompt for Web Navigation using Large Language Models](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2310-18331/) — 2023-10-20
  AllTogether standardizes prompts for HTML-based web navigation and compares prompting with instruction fine-tuning. Its analysis examines how HTML context, interaction history, and current environmental feedback affect task execution.
- [AXNav: Replaying Accessibility Tests from Natural Language](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2310-02424/) — 2023-10-03
  Introduces an LLM and pixel-based UI system that executes natural-language accessibility tests, records navigable videos and flags potential interface issues.
- [GUI-Based Software Testing: An Automated Approach Using GPT-4 and Selenium WebDriver](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-ffc05c4425f777924940/) — 2023-09-11
  A web GUI testing method combines GPT-4 with Selenium WebDriver and compares its branch coverage with random monkey testing on a web calculator.
- [AgentBench: Evaluating LLMs as Agents](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2308-03688/) — 2023-08-07
  Evaluates language agents across eight interactive environments and analyzes failures in reasoning, decisions, and instruction following.
- [WebArena: A Realistic Web Environment for Building Autonomous Agents](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2307-13854/) — 2023-07-25
  Introduces reproducible web environments with task-completion checks for evaluating autonomous web agents.
- [Android in the Wild: A Large-Scale Dataset for Android Device Control](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2307-10088/) — 2023-07-19
  Collects Android interaction demonstrations with screens, actions, and instructions across device and platform variations for robustness evaluation.
- [Mind2Web: Towards a Generalist Agent for the Web](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2306-06070/) — 2023-06-09
  Introduces a web-task dataset spanning diverse websites and evaluates generalization to unseen sites and domains.
- [Responsible Task Automation: Empowering Large Language Models as Responsible Task Automators](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2306-01242/) — 2023-06-02
  Presents ResponsibleTA, a coordinator-executor framework that checks command feasibility and task completion while using local memory for privacy-related controls. The paper compares prompted and learned implementations and evaluates them on UI task automation.
- [SheetCopilot: Bringing Software Productivity to the Next Level through Large Language Models](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2305-19308/) — 2023-05-30
  Introduces SheetCopilot, an agent that steers spreadsheet software via atomic virtual APIs, along with a 221-task spreadsheet manipulation benchmark.
- [AdaPlanner: Adaptive Planning from Feedback with Language Models](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2305-16653/) — 2023-05-26
  Introduces AdaPlanner, which revises code-structured plans in response to environmental feedback and reuses successful plans as exemplars. Experiments include MiniWoB++ web tasks alongside ALFWorld.
- [Mobile User Interface Element Detection Via Adaptively Prompt Tuning](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2305-09699/) — 2023-05-16
  Introduces MUI-zh and APT for detecting mobile UI elements, combining visual features with OCR descriptions to adjust category prompts in CLIP-based detectors.
- [Mobile-Env: Building Qualified Evaluation Benchmarks for LLM-GUI Interaction](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2305-08144/) — 2023-05-14
  Provides an Android benchmark toolkit with controlled environments, intermediate feedback, and task sets for reproducible GUI-agent evaluation.
- [ReAct: Synergizing Reasoning and Acting in Language Models](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2210-03629/) — 2022-10-06
  Introduces ReAct, a method that interleaves reasoning traces and actions for language-model agents; WebShop is one of its interactive decision-making evaluations.
- [ScreenQA: Large-Scale Question-Answer Pairs over Mobile App Screenshots](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2209-08199/) — 2022-09-16
  Introduces screenshot question answering over mobile interfaces, linking answers to screen content and bounding boxes.
- [WebShop: Towards Scalable Real-World Web Interaction with Grounded Language Agents](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2207-01206/) — 2022-07-04
  Provides a simulated shopping environment where language agents search, configure, and select products from natural-language requirements.
- [A Dataset for Interactive Vision-Language Navigation with Unknown Command Feasibility](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2202-02312/) — 2022-02-04
  Introduces MoTIF mobile-app interactions with feasible and infeasible commands, feasibility explanations, and follow-up questions for ambiguous requests.
- [Mobile App Tasks with Iterative Feedback (MoTIF): Addressing Task Feasibility in Interactive Visual Environments](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2104-08560/) — 2021-04-17
  Introduces MoTIF, a dataset of natural-language requests for interactive mobile apps, including infeasible requests and follow-up questions for studying task feasibility.
- [WebSRC: A Dataset for Web-Based Structural Reading Comprehension](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2101-09465/) — 2021-01-23
  Introduces question answering over webpage text and structure, with aligned HTML, screenshots, and metadata for structural reading comprehension.
- [30 Years of Automated GUI Testing: A Bibliometric Analysis](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-24a139a7910431441e39/) — 2021
  A bibliometric review traces automated GUI testing research from 1990 to 2020, examining publication growth, citation patterns, collaboration, and emerging mobile testing topics.
- [GUI testing for mobile applications: objectives, approaches and challenges](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-1c0a2ce1faf3017fa0c5/) — 2020-11
  A systematic review of mobile app GUI testing examines objectives, methods, evaluation metrics, and research challenges across 75 studies published from 2010 to 2020.
- [Object Detection for Graphical User Interface: Old Fashioned or Deep Learning or a Combination?](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-2008-05132/) — 2020-08-12
  Compares seven traditional and learned GUI element detectors on more than 50,000 interface images. The analysis motivates a coarse-to-fine non-text detector combined with a learned text detector to address GUI-specific localization requirements.
- [A Systematic Literature Review of Automated Techniques for Functional GUI Testing of Mobile Applications](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-1812-11470/) — 2018-12-30
  Surveys automated functional GUI testing techniques for mobile apps, comparing the effectiveness, efficiency and practicality of approaches reported in 25 studies.
- [Mapping Natural Language Commands to Web Elements](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-1808-09132/) — 2018-08-28
  Defines language grounding on webpages as choosing the UI element targeted by a natural-language command. A collection of more than 50,000 commands and three baseline models study functional references, relationships between elements, and visual position cues.
- [World of Bits: An Open-Domain Platform for Web-Based Agents](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-52a3261eb6a19b402e88/) — 2017-07-17
  Introduces World of Bits, a web-task platform combining human demonstrations, keyboard and mouse actions, and cached HTTP traffic for repeatable task environments. It evaluates agents trained through behavioral cloning and reinforcement learning.
- [End-to-End Goal-Driven Web Navigation](https://awesomecua.com/resources/arxiv-1602-02261/) — 2016-02-06
  Introduces WebNav for converting websites into goal-directed hyperlink navigation tasks and WikiNav for evaluating language-conditioned navigation and question answering on Wikipedia.
- [GUI Testing Using Computer Vision](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-6262c757080f8838e62c/) — 2010-04
  Presents visual GUI test scripts that use images to specify actions and expected screen feedback. Recording both input events and screenshots can generate tests from demonstrations, supporting unit, regression, and test-driven workflows.

## 评测基准

- [Action Check WebMCP](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-ajaknumber4-action-check-webmcp/) — Date not recorded
  External state-verification benchmark testing whether WebMCP browser tools actually produced their claimed DOM and backend effects.
- [action-choice-bench](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-gbesse-action-choice-bench/) — Date not recorded
  action-choice-bench evaluates discrete UI action selection and calibrated abstention on frozen interface states.
- [Agent Tool Benchmark](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-alloevil-agent-tool-benchmark/) — Date not recorded
  Provides reproducible same-machine benchmarks comparing AI agent browser and desktop toolkits with full execution transcripts.
- [agent-bench](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-megaurav4-agent-bench/) — Date not recorded
  agent-bench evaluates browser-based AI agents across 50 web tasks spanning navigation, form filling, search, and e-commerce checkout.
- [Agent-CE](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-anaishowland-agent-ce/) — Date not recorded
  Agent-CE is a containerized continuous evaluation platform for benchmarking web browsing agents across reproducible task suites.
- [Agentalyze](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-ilyat9-agentalyze/) — Date not recorded
  Agentalyze is an evaluation harness for LLM browser agents with real Chromium tasks, DOM-based state verifiers, and step-level trace analysis.
- [agentB](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-beme08-agentb/) — Date not recorded
  agentB is a benchmark for evaluating open web agents on realistic knowledge-work and browser automation tasks.
- [AgentBench](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-thudm-agentbench/) — Date not recorded
  Agent evaluation suite with containerized tasks and a function-calling interface, including operating-system and shopping environments.
- [AgentCIBench](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-d7f35409d35584385097/) — Date not recorded
  Contextual-integrity benchmark for computer-use agents: 117 synthetic multi-app workspace scenarios that pair under-specified requests with must-share and must-not-share lists, plus a 50-scenario live-UI subset.
- [AgentDAM](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-facebookresearch-ai-agent-privacy/) — Date not recorded
  Web-agent benchmark for measuring unnecessary use of sensitive personal information during end-to-end task execution.
- [AgentDojo](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-ethz-spylab-agentdojo/) — Date not recorded
  Python benchmark package for prompt-injection security of tool-calling agents, with workspace, banking, travel and Slack suites, pluggable attacks and defenses, and a script that scores utility and attack success.
- [AgentForge](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-moeenasim-agentforge-v5/) — Date not recorded
  Multi-agent adversarial evaluation and safety-checking laboratory for stress-testing computer-use agents.
- [AgentGauntlet](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-konuktor-agent-gauntlet/) — Date not recorded
  Reliability CI harness for browser agents that runs identical web tasks across mutating DOM conditions to measure resilience.
- [agentic-browser-boundary-tests](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-larrypeseckis-agentic-browser-boundary-tests/) — Date not recorded
  Local test suite of prompt-injection and instruction-authority boundary scenarios with canary checks for AI browsers and web agents.
- [AgentReady](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-williehems-agentready/) — Date not recorded
  Cloud-browser evaluation harness that tests whether an autonomous AI agent can complete a website's primary conversion action.
- [AgentRewardBench](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-mcgill-nlp-agent-reward-bench/) — Date not recorded
  A benchmark and evaluation toolkit for comparing automatic web-agent judges against expert judgments of task success, side effects and repetition.
- [AndroidArena](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-androidarenaagent-androidarena/) — Date not recorded
  Android environment for evaluating language-model-driven agents.
- [AndroidControl-Curated](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-dda8a0f1413a6bc7c26f/) — Date not recorded
  Purified AndroidControl test set that scores predicted clicks against target element boxes and repairs ambiguous or wrong task labels, released as step-level JSON files with screenshot archives.
- [AndroidDaily](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-8697c910c05d48152916/) — Date not recorded
  Android GUI benchmark dataset from Step-GUI containing 3,146 static action evaluation items and 235 end-to-end daily mobile tasks.
- [AndroidLab](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-thudm-android-lab/) — Date not recorded
  Reproducible Android benchmark of 138 tasks across nine apps on predefined virtual devices, run via AVD on Mac or Docker on Linux, released with the Android Instruct training set.
- [AndroidWorld](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-google-research-android-world/) — Date not recorded
  Android environment with programmatic task evaluation for mobile agents.
- [AppSim-Bench](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-acrab-agentic-labs-appsim/) — Date not recorded
  Simulated mobile applications and evaluation tooling for reproducible GUI-agent tasks with controlled application state.
- [AppWorld](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-stonybrooknlp-appworld/) — Date not recorded
  Simulated applications, user data, and state-based task checks for evaluating agents that interact through APIs and code.
- [AssistantBench](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-oriyor-assistantbench/) — Date not recorded
  AssistantBench evaluates web agents on realistic, time-consuming tasks. Its project repository links to the task dataset and submission leaderboard.
- [Astra Game Arena](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-gih10012-astra-game-arena/) — Date not recorded
  Resumable, screen-only computer-use evaluation arena testing whether Codex agents can play locally installed desktop games.
- [AutomotiveUI-Bench-4K](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-e80e9cf4f565f9ab2abf/) — Date not recorded
  Offline test set of 998 photos and screenshots of in-vehicle infotainment screens from 15 brands, with 4,208 English annotations for locating UI elements and judging expected results as passed or failed.
- [B-MoCA](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-jylee425-b-moca/) — Date not recorded
  Mobile control benchmark with varied interface configurations.
- [Bananalyzer](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-reworkd-bananalyzer/) — Date not recorded
  Bananalyzer is an evaluation framework and dataset suite for testing web agents on structured data extraction and navigation tasks.
- [Baseerat](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-yas1i-baseerat/) — Date not recorded
  Baseerat benchmarks non-visual oversight of computer-use agents, testing whether screen-reader audio descriptions expose unsafe agent actions.
- [BearCubs](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-86229ebd22143128bcf7/) — Date not recorded
  A live-web benchmark with 111 questions testing agents' ability to browse and extract factual answers through text and multimodal interactions.
- [Bellwether](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-al-mohad-bell-weather/) — Date not recorded
  Bellwether measures multi-trial pass^k consistency of computer-use agents on legacy enterprise UI workflows.
- [Bond Underwriting Agent Benchmark](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-vitoworleone-bond-underwriting-agent-benchmark/) — Date not recorded
  Chinese vertical long-horizon evaluation benchmark testing agents on multi-step financial bond-underwriting document and web workflows.
- [BrowseComp](https://awesomecua.com/resources/component-openai-simple-evals-browsecomp/) — Date not recorded
  OpenAI's 1,266-question benchmark for browsing agents, whose short, verifiable answers require persistent search and navigation across many websites and are graded by a model judge.
- [Browser Agent Comparison (4 Approaches)](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-ismael-joffroy-chandoutis-browser-agent-comparison/) — Date not recorded
  Compares four AI browser-control paradigms (Chrome extension, Playwright, Browser Use V3, and CLI) on task completion and cost.
- [Browser Automation Benchmark (Python vs Rust)](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-tashonbraganca-browser-automation-benchmark/) — Date not recorded
  Benchmarks Python Playwright, Rust playwright-rs, and chromiumoxide on headless browser startup, navigation, and screenshot latency.
- [Browser LLM Perf](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-dtopuzov-browser-llm-perf/) — Date not recorded
  Browser LLM Perf compares browser automation CLIs (CraftDriver vs Playwright CLI) on task completion speed and LLM token cost.
- [Browser Use Benchmark (BU Bench)](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-browser-use-benchmark/) — Date not recorded
  Browser Use Benchmark (BU Bench V2.1) evaluates browser agents across 200 real-world web navigation and interaction tasks.
- [browser-eval](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-lexmount-browser-eval/) — Date not recorded
  browser-eval is an evaluation harness for web fetching, deterministic browser trajectory replay, and autonomous web agent benchmarking.
- [browser-task-bench](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-tradestackdev-browser-task-bench/) — Date not recorded
  browser-task-bench is a benchmark suite of 200+ web interaction tasks covering forms, search, authentication, and checkout flows.
- [BrowserAgentRegression](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-albertxxuu-browseragentregression/) — Date not recorded
  Local-first regression testing benchmark for evaluating browser agents against controlled UI and DOM mutations.
- [BrowserART](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-scaleapi-browser-art/) — Date not recorded
  Red-teaming benchmark and test suite from Scale AI evaluating whether refusal-trained language models can be jailbroken when deployed as browser agents.
- [BrowserGym](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-servicenow-browsergym/) — Date not recorded
  Unified interfaces for browser-agent tasks and benchmark environments.
- [BrowserTransactionBench](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-lamenting-hawthorn-browserbench/) — Date not recorded
  BrowserTransactionBench evaluates transactional safety, idempotency, and double-spend prevention in autonomous browser agents.
- [BrowseWebApp Bench](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-visnia-ai-browsewebapp-bench/) — Date not recorded
  BrowseWebApp Bench evaluates browser agents across 100 realistic web workflows spanning record extraction, document upload, and form submission.
- [CAPTCHA Gym](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-mildchef-muse-captcha-gym/) — Date not recorded
  CAPTCHA Gym is a self-hosted evaluation platform for browser agents featuring controlled reCAPTCHA-style visual challenges and server scoring.
- [CaptchaArena](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-x0x0x00-captchaarena/) — Date not recorded
  CaptchaArena provides a live server and fine-grained trajectory dataset across 20 interactive CAPTCHA types for evaluating computer-use agents.
- [ClawBench](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-tiger-ai-lab-clawbench/) — Date not recorded
  Everyday-task benchmark on live websites that runs browser agents in containerized Chromium, blocks each final submission request for scoring by URL match and an LLM judge, and records five layers of run evidence.
- [CodexQA](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-openqa-cn-codexqa/) — Date not recorded
  Suite of 11 local-first QA Agent Skills for Cursor, Claude Code, and Codex to verify live web and desktop user journeys.
- [Collision Canary](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-mystiquemide-collision-canary/) — Date not recorded
  Multi-actor invariant verification harness that drives two concurrent browser agents against a shared backend to uncover race bugs.
- [ColorBench](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-madeagents-colorbench/) — Date not recorded
  A graph-based mobile GUI benchmark that evaluates long tasks through screenshot states and multiple valid or erroneous transitions, including reflection, replanning and backtracking.
- [Computer Agent Arena](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-xlang-ai-computer-agent-arena/) — Date not recorded
  Crowdsourced arena where users pit two computer-use agents against each other on Ubuntu or Windows VMs; the repository holds the voting frontend, setup presets and 2,010 verified battle sessions.
- [CRAB](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-camel-ai-crab/) — Date not recorded
  Cross-environment benchmark framework for multimodal agents.
- [cskwork vigil](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-cskwork-vigil/) — Date not recorded
  Continuous browser QA harness that reproduces reported bugs and autonomously expands end-to-end test coverage on live web URLs.
- [CUA Eval Kit](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-marwanalsiddiq-cua-eval-kit/) — Date not recorded
  Controlled browser fixture and 10-task computer-use evaluation suite for grading web agent accuracy and step efficiency.
- [cua-bench (ERPNext)](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-vaiyr-cua-bench/) — Date not recorded
  cua-bench packages containerized ERPNext v15 accounting and enterprise workflows as Harbor RL and evaluation environments for GUI agents.
- [CUA-Gym-Hub](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-xlang-ai-cua-gym-hub/) — Date not recorded
  CUA-Gym-Hub provides 98 self-contained mock web applications with deterministic state verifiers as RL training and evaluation environments.
- [cua_reliability (SA-OPSD)](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-mercurialsolo-sa-opsd/) — Date not recorded
  Provides the cua_reliability evaluation toolkit and reference training code for measuring stopping and abstention reliability in CUA RL.
- [CUAVerifierBench](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-d5140d438466c1e1348a/) — Date not recorded
  Human-annotated browser-agent trajectories for comparing automated task-success and process-quality verifiers against reviewer judgments.
- [decision-luna-browse-control](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-humanity-forever-decision-luna-browse-control/) — Date not recorded
  Evaluates typed System-One decision models (Luna Decisions and Jev) driving real Chromium browser workflows on synthetic web apps.
- [deedchain](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-pillcrew-deedchain/) — Date not recorded
  deedchain benchmarks report fidelity and hallucination rates of browser agents against recorded ground-truth web execution traces.
- [DeskCert CLI](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-rudrendupaul-deskcert-cli/) — Date not recorded
  Safety certification CLI that stress-tests AI browser and computer-use agents against internal web apps before production deployment.
- [DeskMind Bench](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-deskmind-ai-bench/) — Date not recorded
  DeskMind Bench provides sandboxed macOS desktop tasks, state graders, and reference trajectories for evaluating computer-use agents.
- [DesktopAgentBench](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-raghava00001-desktopagentbench/) — Date not recorded

- [done-or-not](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-tatra-labs-done-or-not/) — Date not recorded

  DeepSeek Harness QA plugin where an autonomous browser agent explores live web applications to discover functional and UX bugs.

- [Graph-Guided Agentic Testing](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-avikundal-graph-guided-agentic-testing/) — Date not recorded
  Uses a browser agent to explore web checkout flows into a Neo4j state graph and synthesizes graph-guided end-to-end tests.
- [GroundUI-18K](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-d206aacf01e1b7dc786d/) — Date not recorded
