  BrowserGym-based benchmark for evaluating the safety and policy compliance of enterprise web agents across six trustworthiness risk dimensions.
- [SWE-bench Multimodal](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-cb7f3bdb832d533c96b0/) — Date not recorded
  Issue-resolution benchmark for visual JavaScript and TypeScript software, where each task's issue or patch includes screenshots, mockups or renderings and fixes are checked by repository unit tests.
- [SWEeper-Bench](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-zlab-princeton-sweeper-bench/) — Date not recorded
  SWEeper-Bench evaluates whether interactive GUI and browser agents can autonomously discover and reproduce 200 real bugs in open-source software.
- [taskproof](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-taskproof-taskproof/) — Date not recorded
  taskproof is an open-source CI harness that tests whether real AI computer-use and browser agents can complete tasks on live websites.
- [TestPilot](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-h0ngza1-testpilot/) — Date not recorded
  Agentic end-to-end web testing platform that executes natural-language test cases in live browsers.
- [TheAgentCompany](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-theagentcompany-theagentcompany/) — Date not recorded
  Self-contained workplace environment for evaluating agents on professional tasks involving websites, files, code, and simulated coworkers.
- [TimeWarp](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-sparklabutah-timewarp/) — Date not recorded
  Web-agent benchmark on self-hosted Wiki, News and Shop sites, each with six era-styled UI versions, registering 231 BrowserGym tasks scored mainly by deterministic answer verifiers.
- [TurkingBench](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-808a22468ee0bf565d6e/) — Date not recorded
  A web-agent benchmark built from crowdsourcing HTML tasks, with 32.2K instances across 158 tasks and a framework that applies model responses to page controls.
- [UI-FaultLab](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-kakoyvostorg-ui-faultlab/) — Date not recorded
  UI-FaultLab benchmarks auditable agent-versus-application failure attribution in visual UI testing workflows.
- [UI-I2E-Bench](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-c08566be0d973ec40c48/) — Date not recorded
  GUI grounding benchmark from UI-E2I-Synth: 1,477 explicit and implicit instructions on mobile, desktop and web screenshots, each paired with a target element's bounding box and element type.
- [UI-NEXUS](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-starwalkin-ui-nexus/) — Date not recorded
  Compositional mobile-agent benchmark: 100 task templates chaining actions across local utility apps on an AndroidWorld-based emulator and online Chinese and English service apps, with emulator state-setup tooling.
- [UniClawBench](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-hku-mmlab-uniclawbench/) — Date not recorded
  UniClawBench evaluates agents on tool, browser, file, and desktop GUI workflows. Its bilingual release contains 400 tasks, Docker runtimes, and trace review tools.
- [UserZero](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-aafre-userzero/) — Date not recorded
  Synthetic first-user evaluation harness that verifies web applications by attempting onboarding and core flows with AI browser agents.
- [Verisim](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-clay-good-verisim/) — Date not recorded
  Verisim is a coverage certifier and adversarial evaluation suite for testing pre-execution guardrails on computer-use and tool agents.
- [VeriWeb (formerly VeriGUI)](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-verigui-team-verigui/) — Date not recorded
  Verifiable web information-seeking benchmark renamed from VeriGUI; desktop evaluation is described as future work.
- [VideoGUI](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-showlab-videogui/) — Date not recorded
  Benchmark for desktop GUI tasks derived from instructional videos.
- [VideoWebArena](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-ljang0-videowebarena/) — Date not recorded
  VideoWebArena evaluates web agents that use video tutorials to complete website tasks. Its released benchmark includes task configurations, environments, and an evaluation pipeline.
- [VisualAgentBench](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-thudm-visualagentbench/) — Date not recorded
  Evaluation framework spanning visual GUI, embodied, and design tasks, including mobile and WebArena-based environments.
- [VisualWebArena](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-web-arena-x-visualwebarena/) — Date not recorded
  Visually grounded web tasks extending the WebArena evaluation setting.
- [VisualWebBench](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-visualwebbench-visualwebbench/) — Date not recorded
  Evaluation of multimodal models on visual web understanding tasks.
- [Voice & Computer-Use Benchmark Landscape](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-sophon-llc-voice-agent-benchmark-landscape/) — Date not recorded
  Structured taxonomy and comparative map of public voice-agent, multimodal, and computer-use evaluation benchmarks.
- [WAInjectBench](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-norrrrrrr-lyn-wainjectbench/) — Date not recorded
  WAInjectBench benchmarks prompt injection detection in web agents across six text and image attack types targeting web interaction pipelines.
- [WATCHER](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-aaman-911-watcher/) — Date not recorded
  Prompt-injection defense classifier, evaluation benchmark, and defended browser harness for autonomous web agents.
- [WeaveBench](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-weavebench-weavebench/) — Date not recorded
  WeaveBench evaluates computer-use agents on long-horizon real-world workflows across hybrid GUI, web, and programmatic tool interfaces.
- [WeaveBench](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-6a212bc12f82c824f6b9/) — Date not recorded
  Computer-use benchmark mixing graphical interaction and code execution.
- [Web Agent Comparison](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-pleasedodisturb-web-agent-comparison/) — Date not recorded
  Reproducible 8-dimension benchmark comparing 7 browser-automation MCP servers on latency, token overhead, and task success.
- [Web Context Bench (Python)](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-charlypoly-web-context-bench-python/) — Date not recorded
  Local-page benchmark comparing six browser representations on token use, factual reading and target-element selection, with deterministic answer scoring.
- [web-agent-bench](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-nsosio-web-agent-bench/) — Date not recorded
  web-agent-bench compares browser-automation harnesses (Playwright MCP, agent-browser, Stagehand) on token usage, latency, and task completion.
- [web-agent-eval (REAL Benchmark Runner)](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-saarthak-yadav-web-agent-eval/) — Date not recorded
  Evaluates web agents on 102 replica websites from the REAL benchmark under explicit step, token, and budget constraints.
- [WebArena](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-web-arena-x-webarena/) — Date not recorded
  Self-hosted web environments and tasks for evaluating autonomous web agents.
- [WebArena-Infinity](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-web-arena-x-webarena-infinity/) — Date not recorded
  Generated web apps modeled on GitLab, PayPal, Xero, Elation EHR and other products, each with resettable state and Python-verified tasks for browser agents, plus the coding-agent pipeline that builds them.
- [WebArena-Verified](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-servicenow-webarena-verified/) — Date not recorded
  An audited re-release of WebArena's 812 web tasks with corrected reference answers and deterministic evaluators that score structured agent responses and captured network traces, plus a 258-task hard subset.
- [WebBench](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-halluminate-webbench/) — Date not recorded
  Live-web benchmark from Halluminate and Skyvern with read, create, update, delete and file-download tasks on 452 high-traffic websites, including logins and form entry, with released per-agent run results.
- [WebCanvas](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-imeanai-webcanvas/) — Date not recorded
  Framework and evaluation resources for real-world web agents.
- [WebClick](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-5e2bd1b3398974b79c9c/) — Date not recorded
  A web-interface localization benchmark with screenshot instructions and target boxes, including agent browsing, human browsing and calendar cases.
- [WebGames](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-convergence-ai-webgames/) — Date not recorded
  150 client-side web challenges in five capability domains, built to be easy for humans but hard for browsing agents, each revealing a password when solved.
- [WebGen-Bench](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-mnluzimu-webgen-bench/) — Date not recorded
  WebGen-Bench evaluates generated websites with automated tests, including a UI agent that carries out interactions and checks outcomes. The repository supplies evaluation code and data.
- [WebGuard](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-osu-nlp-group-webguard/) — Date not recorded
  Codebase, annotation toolkit, and action-level risk dataset for training and evaluating guardrail models that flag unsafe browser-agent actions before execution.
- [WebLINX](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-mcgill-nlp-weblinx/) — Date not recorded
  Demonstrations and evaluation for conversational web navigation.
- [WebLINX 1.1 (WebLINX-BrowserGym)](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-77afb3a9b045a157167c/) — Date not recorded
  WebLINX 1.1, a reprocessed release of WebLINX's conversational web-navigation demonstrations packaged as single-step BrowserGym tasks that score one predicted browser action against the recorded turn.
- [WebMall](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-wbsg-uni-mannheim-webmall/) — Date not recorded
  WebMall is a multi-shop e-commerce web agent benchmark evaluating cross-site price comparison, product search, and cart checkout.
- [WebMCP Description Benchmarks](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-clouatre-labs-webmcp-description-benchmarks/) — Date not recorded
  WebMCP Description Benchmarks evaluates how in-browser WebMCP tool schema descriptions affect agent tool selection and execution accuracy.
- [WebRewardBench](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-ac6d0f80765f5a32255a/) — Date not recorded
  An offline preference-evaluation set for web process reward models, pairing page and trajectory context with preferred and rejected candidate decisions.
- [WebShop](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-princeton-nlp-webshop/) — Date not recorded
  Simulated shopping environment for instruction-following web agents.
- [WebsiteBench](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-tiger-ai-lab-websitebench/) — Date not recorded
  Website reconstruction benchmark: agents explore a production-level site through a browser, rebuild a full-stack version, and are scored by hidden functional and visual test cases across 70 tasks.
- [WebTailBench](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-3b6dfd52f98bfe76bc7f/) — Date not recorded
  Live-web task definitions and rubrics for evaluating computer-use agents, with preserved original tasks, a refreshed V2 task suite and a separate refusal set.
- [WebTestBench](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-friedrichor-webtestbench/) — Date not recorded
  An end-to-end web-testing benchmark where agents derive test checklists from development instructions, interact with deployed applications and report functional or constraint-related defects.
- [WebVoyager](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-minorjerry-webvoyager/) — Date not recorded
  Multimodal web navigation agent and evaluation tasks on real websites.
- [WebWalker](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-alibaba-nlp-webwalker/) — Date not recorded
  Benchmark and multi-agent framework for evaluating large language models on multi-step web traversal and deep information-seeking tasks.
- [WildClawBench](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-internlm-wildclawbench/) — Date not recorded
  WildClawBench evaluates agents on 60 tasks in a live tool environment that includes browser use, email, files and coding. It releases evaluation code and links to task data and trajectories.
- [WinDeskGround](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-zzzhr-1-windeskground/) — Date not recorded
  WinDeskGround benchmarks desktop GUI grounding in multi-window Windows environments with controlled occlusion, scaling, and interference levels.
- [Windows Agent Arena](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-microsoft-windowsagentarena/) — Date not recorded
  Windows 11 benchmark for multimodal desktop agents in a reproducible VM that runs locally or in parallel on Azure ML, with a harder mode where agents must set tasks up themselves.
- [WindowsAgentArena-V2](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-gair-nlp-windowsagentarena-v2/) — Date not recorded
  Windows benchmark revision with task/evaluator fixes and VM-initialization support, documented alongside PC-Agent-E.
- [WindowsWorld](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-hitsz-tmg-windowsworld/) — Date not recorded
  Cross-application Windows benchmark with intermediate task evaluation.
- [WONDERBREAD](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-hazyresearch-wonderbread/) — Date not recorded
  An offline benchmark of workflow documentation, quality assessment and knowledge transfer using human web-navigation demonstrations, action traces and written procedures.
- [WorkArena](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-servicenow-workarena/) — Date not recorded
  Browser-based enterprise workflow tasks built around ServiceNow.
- [WorkArena-Instances](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-01b9e39cd2526d41c527/) — Date not recorded
  A restricted-access set of ServiceNow instances for running WorkArena benchmark evaluations. The repository provides instance information and states research-use conditions.
- [WorldGUI](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-showlab-worldgui/) — Date not recorded
  611 desktop GUI tasks that agents must complete from varied starting points, shipped with instructional videos and project files, plus the self-reflective WorldGUI-Agent baseline.
- [Wornpage WebMCP Conformance](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-wornpage-webmcp-conformance/) — Date not recorded
  Validation toolkit checking page-owned WebMCP tool schemas, authorization boundaries, and execution conformance.

## 数据集

- [Agent Humanization Benchmark (AHB) recordings](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-3e1a864c46c8ed5735d4/) — Date not recorded
  Touch-gesture and motion-sensor recordings of people and mobile GUI agents doing the same Chinese-language Android app tasks, with task lists and metadata for training detectors that tell agents from humans.
- [AgentRewardBench dataset](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-866251b68d6bba8b8ba6/) — Date not recorded
  Hugging Face dataset release for AgentRewardBench containing trajectory evaluation instances across WebArena, VisualWebArena, AssistantBench, WorkArena, and OSWorld.
- [android_control_test](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-1f248862e4b691b85343/) — Date not recorded
  android_control_test packages a processed Android Control test set for evaluating the InfiGUI-R1 mobile GUI agent.
- [bevaya/RICO-ScreenQA-Complex](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-b6114625f883ec0fda33/) — Date not recorded
  A test dataset of complex questions about RICO interface screens, with screen images and ground-truth answers. The card lists 11,781 examples.
- [Browser Agent Failure Corpus](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-mutantweb-browser-agent-failure-corpus/) — Date not recorded
  Browser Agent Failure Corpus is a versioned dataset of browser-agent action failures, execution traces, and guardrail counterexamples.
- [BrowserART](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-dec73112f4c2dad889cb/) — Date not recorded
  A browser-agent safety dataset of 100 harmful browser-related behaviors, designed for red-team evaluation across real and synthetic websites.
- [ClawBench](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-9c96a767b56ba0eb4494/) — Date not recorded
  A browser-agent task corpus for everyday work on live websites, with V1 and V2 test splits, task context, and schemas that intercept final submissions.
- [ClawGUI-Eval](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-e8cea6261ab19611ad23/) — Date not recorded
  GUI grounding evaluation data for six benchmarks, with images, prompts, and prior outputs for an inference, judging, and metrics workflow.
- [CUA Speedrun results](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-099451a33699510476df/) — Date not recorded
  Trajectory archives and per-task results from CUA Speedrun evaluations of computer-use models on OSWorld subsets, CUA-World and MyPCBench, with verifier scores, measured task times and recorded or estimated costs.
- [cua-ai/cua-s1-forms](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-62d912811f786f787ae4/) — Date not recorded
  Training and evaluation examples for selecting GUI form actions from document content, with synthetic train, validation, and test splits plus real browser form demos.
- [CUA-Gym](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-6aa1aa58a7a888395189/) — Date not recorded
  Computer-use environment and task resources for agent research.
- [duvo-eye-1-evals](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-dfcb24f23a03e58316cf/) — Date not recorded
  Dataset of per-sample GUI grounding predictions and reference points for public benchmarks, accompanied by a script for rescoring model outputs.
- [EnvDistraction](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-xbmxb-envdistraction/) — Date not recorded
  EnvDistraction provides simulated GUI environments and evaluation scripts for measuring multimodal agent susceptibility to environmental distractions.
- [Fanbin/waa_steve_trajectories](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-2bab4b78c81cde1d5ecb/) — Date not recorded
  A dataset of STEVE-R1 evaluation trajectories on WindowsAgentArena, pairing task screenshots and agent responses with action traces and outcome labels.
- [Gelato OS-World agent trajectories](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-aed610c413d6bb9f1caa/) — Date not recorded
  Gelato-30B-A3B and GTA1-32B OS-World agent runs, three trials each in the same GPT-5-planned GTA1.5-based harness, with per-task trajectories, initial and step screenshots, and automated results.
- [go-browse-wa-raw](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-edb0eadcc3df5559e941/) — Date not recorded
  go-browse-wa-raw is a browser-agent trajectory dataset with page observations, actions, goals, rewards, and images. Its train split contains 196462 examples.
- [GUI-R1 data bundle](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-df680385f18b01a825be/) — Date not recorded
  Training and evaluation Parquet bundles accompanying GUI-R1, including GUI grounding and mobile/web action-prediction data.
- [GUI-World data](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-40ce19527f25bfa3bd63/) — Date not recorded
  Hub release of GUI-World: GUI screen-recording videos from six scenarios (Android, iOS, website, software, multi-app and XR) with train and benchmark annotation JSONL for GUI-oriented video question answering.
- [Holo4 Trajectories](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-b5dab1881ac1069e44ce/) — Date not recorded
  Dataset of 7,366 computer-use agent execution trajectories from Holo4 27B and Holo4 35B-A3B, recording step-level reasoning, actions, tool results, and screenshots.
- [McGill-NLP/safearena](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-be4ca14d1724b0cf8314/) — Date not recorded
  SafeArena supplies safe and harmful task splits for evaluating the safety of autonomous web agents, with accompanying environment archives under gated access.
- [MemGUI-Bench agent trajectories](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-2b7363c3a20d8022b319/) — Date not recorded
  Recorded MemGUI-Bench runs of mobile GUI agents and models, packaged as per-agent ZIP archives with step screenshots, action logs and evaluator outputs, plus compressed trajectory previews for the leaderboard viewer.
- [Mind2Web 2 task data and judge scripts](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-4f4c4f0bf0cec93c59d1/) — Date not recorded
  Auto-gated Hub release of Mind2Web 2: dev and test task CSVs for long-horizon agentic web search, plus 130 per-task Python evaluation scripts that act as its judge agents.
- [Mind2Web bbox eval (MultiUI)](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-56b5936190d20fa3d1c1/) — Date not recorded
  Mind2Web steps repackaged by the MultiUI authors as JPEG screenshots with per-step JSONL queries and candidate-element boxes, for MultiUI's screenshot-based Mind2Web action evaluation.
- [Mind2Web-Live](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-03ffa3f28413bc2edb1b/) — Date not recorded
  Task files for the WebCanvas live-web benchmark: 438 training and 104 test tasks derived from Mind2Web, each with key-node checks on URLs or page elements that score intermediate progress.
- [Multimodal-Mind2Web](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-66f780b493156dc7efe5/) — Date not recorded
  Mind2Web action steps aligned with the matching webpage screenshot, plus raw and cleaned HTML, candidate elements and the target operation, in a train split and test splits held out by task, website and domain.
- [NAIL-Group/ClawBench](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-146edbaee6701bdb6ee0/) — Date not recorded
  NAIL-Group/ClawBench releases task data for evaluating browser agents on live websites. Its card describes V1 and V2 corpora, task metadata, and loading through the datasets library.
- [NAIL-Group/ClawBenchV1Trace](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-e7653b062bded670198a/) — Date not recorded
  ClawBench V1 Traces releases browser-agent run records with screen video, actions, network traffic and agent messages for replay, debugging and regrading.
- [Online-Mind2Web task data](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-a252b36bf1c440269a2e/) — Date not recorded
  Gated Hub release of the Online-Mind2Web task set: one JSON test split of tasks on live websites, 300 tasks on 136 sites per the project README, which the publishers revise when sites change.
- [OpenGVLab/MMBench-GUI](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-339e28b1f939ae45f9fe/) — Date not recorded
  MMBench-GUI releases annotations and offline images for evaluating interface understanding and grounding within a GUI-agent benchmark spanning desktop, mobile and web platforms.
- [OSWorld 2.0 task classes](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-1c4a0389ace7c5cb8a4b/) — Date not recorded
  Gated Hub release of the OSWorld 2.0 benchmark's Python task classes, which hold task setup and evaluator logic kept out of the public code to limit leakage and are pinned per benchmark release.
- [OSWorld 2.0 trajectories](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-3b4e6c30e1d095c22771/) — Date not recorded
  XLang's gated release of OSWorld 2.0 agent runs: 21 ZIP result archives named by model and run settings, plus per-task trajectory JSONL, step screenshots, results and logs for seven model runs.
- [OSWorld file cache (Ubuntu)](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-c885712b9c69579ac591/) — Date not recorded
  Hub-hosted copy of the files that OSWorld's Ubuntu task configurations download for task setup and evaluation, organized by application and task ID after their migration from Google Drive.
- [OSWorld-Science task data](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-263e82b29d4fca964463/) — Date not recorded
  Gated Hub release of OSWorld-Science task definitions, staged inputs, grader references and prepared Ubuntu VM images for computer-use agents working in scientific software.
- [OSWorld-V2 0624 Codex attempt trajectories](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-9112dfcb201f2de7241f/) — Date not recorded
  Self-reported OSWorld-V2 run of a Codex-based harness with gpt-5.6-sol on all 108 tasks: per-step CLI and GUI actions, screenshots and scores, plus retried attempts and the solution and recovery cards that guided them.
- [OSWorld-Verified trajectories](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-2c99f0de2840277abc72/) — Date not recorded
  Archived OSWorld-Verified evaluation runs of many computer-use agents and step budgets on Ubuntu, each archive holding screenshots, actions, model reasoning and per-task results for analysis.
- [RelationGUI & RelationQA](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-malonghui-relationgui/) — Date not recorded
  RelationGUI provides datasets and training code for explicit spatial, hierarchical, and functional relational understanding in GUI agents.
- [RICO-ScreenQA](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-bd15fb092ff80643ed97/) — Date not recorded
  RICO-ScreenQA provides questions and answers for app screenshots, with images, answer text, and UI-element bounds in train, validation, and test splits.
- [Sai on OSWorld v2.1 reference-VM runs](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-a19a076a4808a80f6ac8/) — Date not recorded
  Simular's 134 clean runs of its Sai computer-use agent on the 108 OSWorld v2.1 tasks in a reference Ubuntu VM, with OSWorld trajectories, agent records, harness and grader logs, scorecards and screenshot and video tars.
- [Sai OSWorld-V2 benchmark runs](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-e0180b9651fec04d0308/) — Date not recorded
  Simular's per-task evidence from two complete 108-task OSWorld-V2 runs of its Sai computer-use agent: scores, step trajectories, message traces, evaluator and API logs, and run manifests, without screenshots.
- [ScienceBoard evaluation trajectories](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-4784f63cd7117f64a2a3/) — Date not recorded
  Archives of ScienceBoard agent evaluation runs, separated by agent configuration and screenshot, accessibility-tree or set-of-marks observation mode.
- [Screen Point-and-Read Data](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-4b747a376f628e6f9760/) — Date not recorded
  Screen Point-and-Read Data pairs OS, web and mobile screenshots with selected points, annotated regions and corrected captions for layout-aware GUI interpretation.
- [ScreenDrag](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-fe43af1801032be708cc/) — Date not recorded
  GUI dragging data with screen observations, prior state, and continuous pointer actions, provided as trajectory files and Parquet representations.
- [ScreenSpot-Pro data](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-c79e152e6f7af1a9efed/) — Date not recorded
  High-resolution professional interface screenshots and target annotations for evaluating instruction-based GUI localization.
- [TreeCUA-Datasets](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-2e8bba756c71f9fe3fbd/) — Date not recorded
  TreeCUA-Datasets releases GUI exploration trees with setup files, action sequences, goals, and verification results. Users replay the trees in OSWorld to reconstruct screen observations.
- [UI-AGILE-Data](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-41abb1b7206689cf5057/) — Date not recorded
  UI-AGILE training annotations and screenshots together with converted GUI-grounding and AndroidControl evaluation files.
- [UIBert AppSim and RefExp annotations](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-google-research-datasets-uibert/) — Date not recorded
  Two Rico-derived UI component retrieval datasets: AppSim pairs semantically similar elements across screens, while RefExp links referring expressions to target elements.
- [UICrit](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-google-research-datasets-uicrit/) — Date not recorded
  Mobile-interface critiques and quality ratings with region coordinates and RICO screen references, including a larger three-annotator release than the original paper dataset.
- [UiPad](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-3ac3ccfb23fe13adf428/) — Date not recorded
  A macOS UI dataset with 352 screens from 63 applications. It pairs screenshots with available accessibility trees and questions about interface elements and click locations.
- [VeriGUI](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-8477ada5b7de1d399c9d/) — Date not recorded
  VeriWeb dataset of 302 web information-seeking tasks, with annotated subtasks, human action logs, and execution videos for evaluating long-chain agents.
- [VeriOS-Bench](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-16a5ccde8bb16cda19ff/) — Date not recorded
  A dataset for training and evaluating GUI-operating OS agents on decisions to ask a person for help when autonomous action could be unreliable.
- [VisualWebBench dataset](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-149769d8bf07b7fbabdc/) — Date not recorded
  Hugging Face dataset release for VisualWebBench spanning seven visual web understanding, OCR, element grounding, and action prediction tasks.
- [VLM-Reasoning-Traces](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-llamatouch-vlm-reasoning-traces/) — Date not recorded
  VLM-Reasoning-Traces releases model outputs comparing base and thinking-enabled vision-language models on AndroidControl, ScreenSpot, and AndroidWorld.
- [WebGuard](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-df10c80a8da55773154d/) — Date not recorded
  Dataset of 5,999 browser-action safety annotations, pairing website and action details with risk classifications for web-agent research.
- [WebPRM Collection preference data](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-8dfc84eb4016204fe70a/) — Date not recorded
  Checklist-annotated web decision records with page observations, prior actions, one preferred continuation and rejected alternatives for process-reward learning.
- [WebSRC](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-x-lance-websrc-baseline/) — Date not recorded
  WebSRC provides baseline models and processing pipelines for the Web-Based Structural Reading Comprehension dataset over HTML and screenshots.
- [websrc](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-d768dd5245832bc4b824/) — Date not recorded
  websrc is a screenshot-focused WebSRC compilation for answering questions about webpage content and structure. It provides page identifiers, questions, answers, and encoded images.
- [WikiHow Task Set](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-25af759a783f1514fe58/) — Date not recorded
  A Mobile-Env task set for evaluating navigation in the WikiHow app, with supplementary tasks across Android apps. It provides task definitions and rewritten instructions.
- [WorldGUI-Bench](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-b540fe81c57cedb1f556/) — Date not recorded
  A desktop GUI evaluation dataset with user requests, instructional videos and pre-actions that vary task starting states. The release includes task metadata and a data archive.

## 模型

- [Claude 3.7 Sonnet](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-63264f7cd4510d96e486/) — Date not recorded
  A Claude release with an optional extended-thinking mode. Anthropic describes its mouse and keyboard computer-use capability and reports OSWorld evaluation results.
- [Claude Opus 5](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-c34a327604b5be4a7c98/) — Date not recorded
  Claude Opus 5 is a general model whose release page reports computer-use performance on OSWorld 2.0 and describes agent work across browser and business tasks.
- [Claude Sonnet 4.5](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-18816edf464a073a0120/) — Date not recorded
  Claude Sonnet 4.5 is a general model available through an API. Anthropic reports its computer-use performance on OSWorld and demonstrates browser tasks.
- [Claude Sonnet 4.6](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-c4a45eed67ee9087aae3/) — Date not recorded
  A general model whose release documents computer use through visual observation, mouse actions, and keyboard input, with OSWorld evaluation and prompt-injection testing.
- [Fintor/Fintor-GUI-S2](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-0b3bb9fac5b0ba9777b8/) — Date not recorded
  Fintor-GUI-S2 is a GUI grounding checkpoint fine-tuned from UI-TARS-7B-DPO on OS-Copilot data. Its card reports ScreenSpot Pro and v2 evaluations.
- [GTA1-7B](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-fa6761a4612fab664a7c/) — Date not recorded
  Released GUI grounding checkpoint trained with reinforcement learning to predict click coordinates from screenshots and instructions, with inference examples and reported benchmark results.
- [GTA1-7B-2507](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-691c52679bb1d3521db7/) — Date not recorded
  GTA1-7B-2507 is a GUI-grounding checkpoint trained with reinforcement learning to locate described elements in screenshots and return click coordinates.
- [Indeed-UI](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-intelligence-indeed-indeed-ui/) — Date not recorded
  A GUI grounding model described for desktop, mobile, and browser interfaces. Its repository provides ScreenSpot-Pro evaluation and zoom-in scripts.
- [Kimi-VL-A3B-Thinking-2506](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-fb5921fec3f7c313ab5b/) — Date not recorded
  A multimodal reasoning model with released weights and high-resolution image input. Its authors report GUI grounding evaluations on ScreenSpot-Pro, ScreenSpot-V2 and OSWorld-G.
- [Llama8b-NNetNav-WA](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-c9d2a9288a568db12bd1/) — Date not recorded
  A Llama checkpoint trained on demonstrations gathered through WebArena exploration. It takes a task instruction and selects browser actions to navigate webpages.
- [Orienter](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-758f048c78e5c184333c/) — Date not recorded
  Orienter locates interactable elements in extended reality interface screenshots using application context and a detector. The release includes code and a checkpoint.
- [Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-52ec96785d9c0ae8e039/) — Date not recorded
  An instruction-tuned vision-language checkpoint that reads visual layouts, localizes points or boxes, and supports computer and phone use. Its card reports screen and mobile-agent evaluations.
- [Qwen2.5-VL-3B-UI-R1-E](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-72f3737bb5194fd64852/) — Date not recorded
  Qwen2.5-VL-3B-UI-R1-E releases UI-R1-E-3B, a model that predicts click coordinates from interface screenshots and commands, with reported mobile, desktop, and web grounding evaluations.
- [Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-557edb1d63e8fcfdee12/) — Date not recorded
  Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct releases vision-language weights whose card describes computer and phone use and reports results on screen grounding and mobile-agent benchmarks.
- [Salesforce/GTA1-32B](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-5288515eb50aef1e84a2/) — Date not recorded
  A 32B GUI grounding checkpoint trained with GRPO to produce screen actions from tasks and screenshots, with reported results on grounding and desktop agent benchmarks.
- [UGround-V1-72B](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-7ea46aa7d2d4470bb1f8/) — Date not recorded
  UGround-V1-72B is a Qwen2-VL-based model for locating targets in GUI screenshots, released with weights and reported evaluations on mobile, desktop, and web screens.
- [WebJudge 7B](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-772161f06c2b3d4978b5/) — Date not recorded
  A visual trajectory judge that retains selected intermediate screenshots to evaluate whether a web agent completed its task.
- [WebRL-GLM-4-9B](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-f979ca583090405aadee/) — Date not recorded
  A GLM-based WebRL model released for browser tasks across five WebArena websites. Its card reports evaluation results on WebArena-Lite.
- [webrl-llama-3.1-70b](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-5371bf588e7790a66ff0/) — Date not recorded
  A released Llama-3.1-70B WebRL model for operating five WebArena websites. Its model card reports WebArena-Lite results and points to inference requirements.

## 工程与产业

- [404.directory](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-mm-sheng-404-directory/) — Date not recorded
  Pre-execution risk preflight service and SDK that checks third-party tools and domains before AI agents invoke them.
- [Action-Type-Conditioned Grounding for GUI Agents](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-aadcha-action-conditioned-gui-agent/) — Date not recorded
  Research code for GUI grounding that predicts an action type and then conditions coordinate prediction on it. It compares six conditioning methods and includes training and evaluation scripts.
- [AdInject](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-nicerwang-adinject/) — Date not recorded
  AdInject implements black-box indirect prompt injection attacks delivered through web advertising banners on OSWorld and VisualWebArena.
- [AdvAgent (formerly AdvWeb)](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-ai-secure-advagent/) — Date not recorded
  Research code for constructing and evaluating adversarial inputs to web agents, with a black-box red-team pipeline using Mind2Web data and SeeAct.
- [Agent DevTools (39-tools)](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-yydongro-39-tools/) — Date not recorded
  Action-level observability, state verification, and debugging toolkit for computer-use agents.
- [Agent Eyes](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-jumpsy-agent-eyes/) — Date not recorded
  Visual inspection toolkit for AI agents that captures rendered screenshots and extracts live CSS design tokens from the DOM.
- [Agent VCR](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-ixchio-agent-vcr/) — Date not recorded
  Agent VCR records, replays, and diffs deterministic cassettes of multi-turn agent tool calls, state transitions, and execution traces.
- [agent-gauntlet-example-agent](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-konuktor-agent-gauntlet-example-agent/) — Date not recorded
  Compact single-file reference browser agent designed to run inside a Solari Sandbox under AgentGauntlet evaluation.
- [agent-qa](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-vostride-agent-qa/) — Date not recorded
  agent-qa is an AI quality-assurance runner that executes natural-language user-journey tests across web, Android, and iOS interfaces.
- [agent-qa](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-73e9e26d40de2a60ae05/) — Date not recorded
  A QA agent that runs plain-language tests against web, Android, and iOS apps. It navigates user flows, verifies outcomes, and stores validated run evidence for reuse.
- [agent-qa](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-f7e21b1a3ba5c58a73bc/) — Date not recorded
  QA agent for browser and mobile apps that executes actions through Playwright and Appium, verifies steps, and records runs. Its publisher reports completing all AndroidWorld tasks.
- [AgentGym](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-woooodyy-agentgym/) — Date not recorded
  AgentGym provides interactive environments for developing and evaluating agents, including web tasks in WebShop and WebArena, with trajectory collection and training support.
- [Agentic Reward Modeling (RewardAgent)](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-thu-keg-agentic-reward-modeling/) — Date not recorded
  RewardAgent combines human-preference scores with verifiable factuality and instruction-following checks to judge agent trajectories.
- [Agentic Web Check](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-ericovirgy-agentic-web-check/) — Date not recorded
  Agentic Web Check is a Lighthouse-style auditor that scores websites across 43 checks for AI browser-agent accessibility and controllability.
- [AgentiTest](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-kweinmeister-agentitest/) — Date not recorded
  AgentiTest is a natural-language browser test automation framework built on Python pytest, Allure reporting, and LLM web agents.
- [AgentLab](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-servicenow-agentlab/) — Date not recorded
  Experiment framework for developing and evaluating browser agents.
- [Agentless](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-openautocoder-agentless/) — Date not recorded
  Agentless resolves software engineering issues through a three-phase localization, repair, and patch validation pipeline.
- [AgentOccam](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-amazon-science-agentoccam/) — Date not recorded
  AgentOccam is a web agent that uses webpage descriptions and action strings to carry out tasks from a URL and instruction. Its repository includes task configurations and evaluation scripts.
- [AgentScrub](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-codewithsupra-agentscrub/) — Date not recorded
  Visual session-replay debugger for Browser Use agent runs that lets developers scrub through step timelines and DOM states.
- [AgentStudio](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-skyworkai-agent-studio/) — Date not recorded
  Toolkit and Ubuntu-based environment for building and evaluating generalist computer-use agents through GUI and API interaction.
- [AGI SDK](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-agi-inc-agisdk/) — Date not recorded
  AGI SDK provides a browser-agent API and evaluation harness for tasks on deterministic replicas of interactive websites, with support for custom agents.
- [AI Medical Claim Form-Filling Agent](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-caicai-jin-ai-medicalclaimform-filling-agent/) — Date not recorded
  LLM-driven browser agent that extracts patient intake data and autonomously fills and submits web medical claim forms.
- [AI Web](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-dongxinsuperman-ai-web/) — Date not recorded
  Natural-language Browser Agent execution platform supporting multi-browser scheduling, task queues, and traceable QA test reports.
- [AI Web Tester](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-siamalsobari-ai-web-tester/) — Date not recorded
  Autonomous browser testing, QA matrix execution, and console/network telemetry toolkit for AI coding agents.
- [AI-Agentic QA Framework](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-daud-khan-qa-ai-agentic-qa-framework/) — Date not recorded
  Zero-dependency CDP browser driver and hydration-reliability testing framework for agentic web QA.
- [Amap-Sim](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-acrab-agentic-labs-appsim-amap/) — Date not recorded
  An offline Android map-app simulation for AI automation tests, with map browsing, place search, routing, and navigation backed by locally prepared data.
- [AndroidWorld-SeeAct-V](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-boyugou-android-world-seeact-v/) — Date not recorded

- [AndroTMem inference and evaluation code](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-cvc2233-androtmem/) — Date not recorded

- [AnimaWatch](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-rishitank-animawatch/) — Date not recorded
  FastMCP server that records webpage animations and uses vision models to detect frame drops, jank, and visual layout artifacts.
- [AOI (Agent-Computer Observation Interface) & DynaCU-Bench](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-19pine-ai-aoi/) — Date not recorded

- [API-Based-Agent](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-yueqis-api-based-agent/) — Date not recorded
  Code for web agents that use application APIs and browser actions together, with scripts for evaluating the hybrid approach on WebArena.
- [Apply Lens](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-mangeshraut712-apply-lens/) — Date not recorded

- [AppSim-UberEats](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-acrab-agentic-labs-appsim-ubereats/) — Date not recorded

- [Appwalk](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-kubstack-appwalk/) — Date not recorded

- [AskUI](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-d071f3f5a01a5f9d6d78/) — Date not recorded
  AskUI runs plain-language Android app tests through visual screen interaction on devices and emulators, with screenshot traces and CI integrations.
- [AUITestAgent](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-bz-lab-auitestagent/) — Date not recorded

- [AutoCodeRover](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-nus-apr-auto-code-rover/) — Date not recorded

- [Autodesk Claude Browser Test Skills](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-autodesk-claude-browser-test-skills/) — Date not recorded

- [Avenir-UX](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-onflow-ai-avenir-ux/) — Date not recorded

- [Camel AI (Visual QA Agent)](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-dilawarshafiq-camel-ai/) — Date not recorded

## 学习资源

- [Achieving 91.4% on Android World: A New Approach to Mobile UI Automation](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-f8b67bde8c79e7934a2a/) — Date not recorded
  Technical article describing an Android UI agent that alternates planning with single actions, checks screen changes and uses a dedicated text editing path. It reports AndroidWorld results.
- [Agentic UI Testing Codelab](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-a-bhimava-agentic-ui-testing/) — Date not recorded
  Hands-on developer codelab for building automated UI test suites using Antigravity CLI and Browser Use.
- [Awesome GPT-6 Astra](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-archorfight-awesome-gpt-6-astra/) — Date not recorded
  Evidence-linked catalog of computer-use demos, evaluations, and agent workflows with primary source receipts.
- [Awesome GUI Agent Security](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-yuxuan2003-awesome-gui-agent-security/) — Date not recorded
  Bilingual curated reading list of GUI, computer-use, and browser agent security papers organized along attack and defense axes.
- [Awesome Multimodal Agentic Coding](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-jiangjin1999-awesome-multimodal-agentic-coding/) — Date not recorded
  Comprehensive research map of papers, benchmarks, and tools for multimodal coding and UI-verifying agents.
- [Awesome Reinforcement Learning for GUI Agents](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-steve2457-awesome-rl-gui-agents/) — Date not recorded
  A curated reading list of reinforcement learning work on GUI agents, organized by learning methods, reward and efficiency questions, datasets, and training environments.
- [NeurIPS 2025: 45 Computer-Use Agent Papers You Should Know About](https://awesomecua.com/resources/url-5f211a7eba35415ea382/) — Date not recorded
  An annotated reading list of 45 NeurIPS 2025 computer-use agent papers, organized around benchmarks, safety, grounding and agent architectures.
- [Open Operator](https://awesomecua.com/resources/github-openhands-open-operator/) — Date not recorded
  Open Operator collects resources on computer-use agents, with task categories, benchmark summaries, and pages describing existing agent systems.
